Artemis/Core/Domain/4_Tables/Object/Type/Specific/ObjectVehicle.ixx	F1	// Target of each seat element, in the order of the tag. 1.0f = free, 0.0f = occupied.
Artemis/Core/Domain/4_Tables/Object/Type/Specific/ObjectVehicle.ixx	F1	// The 'hijacker' seats are written after the normal seats, following the same structure and
Artemis/Core/Domain/4_Tables/Object/Type/Specific/ObjectVehicle.ixx	F1	// The elements follow the order of the seats in the tag, so a seat is found by its index.
Artemis/Core/Domain/4_Tables/Object/Type/Specific/ObjectVehicle.ixx	F1	// The first float is a smoothed value that ramps towards the second one (the target) every tick.
Artemis/Core/Domain/4_Tables/Object/Type/Specific/ObjectVehicle.ixx	F1	// The target is 1.0f when the seat is free and 0.0f when the seat is occupied.
Artemis/Core/Domain/4_Tables/Object/Type/Specific/ObjectVehicle.ixx	F1	// The vehicle datum has a variable block (descriptor 0x560, see Datum.Object.Type:Block) with
Artemis/Core/Domain/4_Tables/Object/Type/Specific/ObjectVehicle.ixx	F1	// We don't know specificly which player, but with other data we get from other sides of this
Artemis/Core/Domain/4_Tables/Object/Type/Specific/ObjectVehicle.ixx	F1	// behaviour. So, we can know whenever a specific seat is being hijacked by another player.
Artemis/Core/Domain/4_Tables/Object/Type/Specific/ObjectVehicle.ixx	F1	// found. The Grenadier of the Falcon had inconsistent offsets, its indices are assumed.
Artemis/Core/Domain/4_Tables/Object/Type/Specific/ObjectVehicle.ixx	F1	// note: The index of each seat was derived from the offsets that were used before the block was
Artemis/Core/Domain/4_Tables/Object/Type/Specific/ObjectVehicle.ixx	F1	// one element of 12 bytes per seat of the vehicle tag: a pair of floats and one static flag '3'.
Artemis/Core/Domain/4_Tables/Object/Type/Specific/ObjectVehicle.ixx	F1	// project, we might be able to do this connection.
